```text
Lyra image viewer codec boundary.

No vendored codec source is committed in this native image viewer landing. The
OpenImageIO bridge is optional and links only when headers and libraries are
provided by the local system or a future bundled dependency. When codec source or
prebuilt codec binaries are added under third-party/native/image-codecs, add the
corresponding upstream license and notice files here.
```

```text
viewer landing. This placeholder records the legal destination for future
vendored permissive image codec licenses.
```
